+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}
+

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}
+

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

The edge-case suite also passes for empty matrices, fully dense matrices, and very sparse matrices, and an AddressSanitizer/UndefinedBehaviorSanitizer run found no memory or arithmetic errors. On a 2048² test, throughput scaled from 1.44 GFLOPS on one rank to 11.42 GFLOPS on eight ranks (about 7.9×), with communication excluded from the repeated SpMV loop as intended.
Implementation and functional testing are complete. Multi-rank hashes match, empty-rank and sparsity extremes pass, sanitizers are clean, and a local scaling run is nearly linear. Performing the final clean configure/build and smoke test.

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

+            const index_t firstRow = rowBoundaries[target];
+            const index_t endRow = rowBoundaries[target + 1];

+    int collectiveDistribution = 1;

+    std::vector<int> rowSendDisplacements;

+        rowSendDisplacements.resize(processCount);
+        nnzSendCounts.resize(processCount);
+        nnzSendDisplacements.resize(processCount);

+                rowBoundaries[target] > static_cast<index_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+                rankNnzCounts[target] > static_cast<uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+                rankNnzDisplacements[target] >
+                    static_cast<uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+                collectiveDistribution = 0;
+                break;
+            }
+            rowSendCounts[target] = static_cast<int>(rows + 1);
+            rowSendDisplacements[target] = static_cast<int>(rowBoundaries[target]);

+                     rank == 0 ? nnzSendDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_UINT32_T,
+                     localCols.data(), static_cast<int>(localNnz64), MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    } else if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int target = 0; target < processCount; ++target) {
+            const size_t rows = static_cast<size_t>(rowBoundaries[target + 1]) -
+                                rowBoundaries[target];
+            const size_t nnz = static_cast<size_t>(rankNnzCounts[target]);
+            const size_t nnzOffset = static_cast<size_t>(rankNnzDisplacements[target]);

+                std::copy_n(globalRowDelimiters.data() + rowBoundaries[target], rows + 1,
+                            localRowDelimiters.data());

+                std::copy_n(globalCols.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, localCols.data());
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowDelimiters.data() + rowBoundaries[target], rows + 1,
+                             MPI_UINT32_T, target, 100, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+                mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, MPI_DOUBLE, target, 101,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+                mpiSendLarge(globalCols.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, MPI_UINT32_T, target, 102,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        mpiRecvLarge(localRowDelimiters.data(), localRowDelimiters.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0,

+        mpiRecvLarge(localValues.data(), localValues.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        mpiRecvLarge(localCols.data(), localCols.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0, 102,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    }

+    for (index_t& delimiter : localRowDelimiters) {
+        delimiter -= firstLocalNnz;
+    }

+    // distributed memory footprint as every other rank (apart from validation data).
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::vector<double>().swap(globalValues);
+        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalCols);
+        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalRowDelimiters);
+    }
+
+    std::vector<double> localOut(localRows);
+

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

+        nnzSendDisplacements.resize(processCount);
+        for (int target = 0; target < processCount; ++target) {
+            const uint64_t rows = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowBoundaries[target + 1]) -
+                                  rowBoundaries[target];
+            if (rows + 1 > static_cast<uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||

+                break;

+            rowSendDisplacements[target] = static_cast<int>(rowBoundaries[target]);

+            nnzSendDisplacements[target] = static_cast<int>(rankNnzDisplacements[target]);
+        }
+    }
+    MPI_Bcast(&collectiveDistribution, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+

+                     rank == 0 ? rowSendCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? rowSendDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_UINT32_T,

+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+                     rank == 0 ? nnzSendCounts.data() : nullptr,
+                     rank == 0 ? nnzSendDisplacements.data() : nullptr, MPI_UINT32_T,
+                     localCols.data(), static_cast<int>(localNnz64), MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    } else if (rank == 0) {
+        for (int target = 0; target < processCount; ++target) {
+            const size_t rows = static_cast<size_t>(rowBoundaries[target + 1]) -
+                                rowBoundaries[target];
+            const size_t nnz = static_cast<size_t>(rankNnzCounts[target]);
+            const size_t nnzOffset = static_cast<size_t>(rankNnzDisplacements[target]);
+            if (target == 0) {
+                std::copy_n(globalRowDelimiters.data() + rowBoundaries[target], rows + 1,
+                            localRowDelimiters.data());
+                std::copy_n(globalValues.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, localValues.data());
+                std::copy_n(globalCols.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, localCols.data());
+            } else {
+                mpiSendLarge(globalRowDelimiters.data() + rowBoundaries[target], rows + 1,
+                             MPI_UINT32_T, target, 100, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+                mpiSendLarge(globalValues.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, MPI_DOUBLE, target, 101,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+                mpiSendLarge(globalCols.data() + nnzOffset, nnz, MPI_UINT32_T, target, 102,
+                             MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        mpiRecvLarge(localRowDelimiters.data(), localRowDelimiters.size(), MPI_UINT32_T, 0,
+                     100, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+        mpiRecvLarge(localValues.data(), localValues.size(), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, 101,
+                     MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+        delimiter -= firstLocalNnz;

+    // distributed memory footprint as every other rank (apart from validation data).
+    if (rank == 0) {

+        std::vector<index_t>().swap(globalRowDelimiters);
+    }

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

codex
Implemented MPI-distributed SpMV while preserving the `spmv` executable name.

- Work-balanced contiguous CSR row decomposition across ranks.
- Dense vector broadcast once; timed iterations require no communication.
- Matrix and output remain distributed; results gather only for `-v` or `-r`.
- Runtime uses the slowest-rank timing for accurate distributed performance.
- Added large-count MPI fallbacks and support for ranks with zero rows.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1) to require and link MPI.
- Main implementation is in [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp:226).

Validation completed:

- Clean Release build succeeds.
- Identical result hashes across 1–8 ranks.
- Dense, empty, sparse, and zero-row partition cases pass.
- Sanitizer checks pass.
- Local 8-rank test achieved approximately 7.9× throughput over one rank.
- No new workspace files were created.

+// Divide contiguous rows so that each rank receives approximately the same
+// number of nonzeros plus rows. Including rows in the cost also balances empty
+// and very sparse matrices.
+std::vector<index_t> partitionRows(const std::vector<index_t>& rowDelimiters,
+                                   const index_t dim, const index_t nnz,
+                                   const int processCount) {
+    std::vector<index_t> boundaries(static_cast<size_t>(processCount) + 1);
+    boundaries.front() = 0;
+    boundaries.back() = dim;
+
+    const uint64_t totalWork = static_cast<uint64_t>(nnz) + dim;
+    for (int rank = 1; rank < processCount; ++rank) {
+        // unsigned __int128 prevents overflow when forming the fractional target.
+        const uint64_t target = static_cast<uint64_t>(
+            (static_cast<unsigned __int128>(totalWork) * rank) / processCount);
+
+        index_t low = boundaries[static_cast<size_t>(rank) - 1];
+        index_t high = dim;
+        while (low < high) {
+            const index_t mid = low + (high - low) / 2;
+            const uint64_t workThroughMid = static_cast<uint64_t>(rowDelimiters[mid]) + mid;
+            if (workThroughMid < target) {
+                low = mid + 1;
+            } else {
+                high = mid;
+            }
+        }
+        boundaries[rank] = low;
+    }
+    return boundaries;
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiBcastLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int root,
+                   MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Bcast(data + offset, chunk, datatype, root, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiSendLarge(const T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int destination,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Send(data + offset, chunk, datatype, destination, tag, communicator);
+    }
+}
+
+template <typename T>
+void mpiRecvLarge(T* data, size_t count, MPI_Datatype datatype, int source,
+                  int tag, MPI_Comm communicator) {
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk = static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < count; offset += maxChunk) {
+        const int chunk = static_cast<int>(std::min(maxChunk, count - offset));
+        MPI_Recv(data + offset, chunk, datatype, source, tag, communicator,
+                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+    }
+}

Implemented MPI-distributed SpMV while preserving the `spmv` executable name.

- Work-balanced contiguous CSR row decomposition across ranks.
- Dense vector broadcast once; timed iterations require no communication.
- Matrix and output remain distributed; results gather only for `-v` or `-r`.
- Runtime uses the slowest-rank timing for accurate distributed performance.
- Added large-count MPI fallbacks and support for ranks with zero rows.
- Updated [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/spmv/CMakeLists.txt:1) to require and link MPI.
- Main implementation is in [spmv.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/spmv_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_mpi_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp:226).
